- **ics46-notes**: Kazuha (third-year CS at UCI, took ICS 45C, codes in Go/Python/C++) wants notes he can study from **instead of** re-reading the slides, and he publishes them on his UCI static site (ics.uci.edu/~kazuhak) for everyone, next to his ICS 45C notes. So the notes must contain everything the slides contain — including what is only in the pictures — written in clear English. ([KKazuhaK/uci.kazuha.me](https://github.com/KKazuhaK/uci.kazuha.me/tree/HEAD/docs/skills/ics46-notes))
- **icscards**: Read an ICS credit card account (International Card Services, icscards.nl, which also issues the ABN AMRO, ANWB and Bijenkorf credit cards): balance, credit limit and spending left, pending payments, the last statement and its direct debit, transactions per statement period with merchant category and foreign amounts, and monthly statement PDFs. Use when the user asks about their credit card balance, bill or limit, what they spent with the credit card, a card transaction, or the monthly ICS statement (rekeningoverzicht). ([ghuron/skills](https://github.com/ghuron/skills/tree/HEAD/icscards) · ★1)
- **icse**: Writing and formatting guidance for ICSE (ACM/IEEE International Conference on Software Engineering). Use when drafting, formatting, and submitting to ICSE 2026. Covers ACM acmart with sigconf format, anonymous double-blind review mode, ICSE page limits (14 pages), software engineering conventions, research and technical papers, theorem environments, code listings, camera-ready preparation with DOI/ISBN, and ACM-Reference-Format citations. ([SimonZeng7108/ccf-conference-skills](https://github.com/SimonZeng7108/ccf-conference-skills/tree/HEAD/ccf-conference-skills/icse) · ★21)
- **icse-artifact-evaluation**: Use when preparing an artifact for the ICSE Artifact Evaluation track after paper acceptance, covering the ACM badge system (Available, Reusable, Results Reproduced/Replicated), evaluator-proof packaging, archival requirements, open licensing, and the January-window timeline anchored to recent cycles. ([brycewang-stanford/Awesome-Journal-Skills](https://github.com/brycewang-stanford/Awesome-Journal-Skills/tree/HEAD/ICSE-Skills/skills/icse-artifact-evaluation) · ★1.4k · 2 copies)
- **icse-author-response**: Use when drafting an ICSE author response or executing a Major Revision, covering criterion-targeted replies to research-track reviews, the September response window, the four-week revision sprint to the November deadline, response-letter structure, and change tracking that survives PC re-review. ([brycewang-stanford/Awesome-Journal-Skills](https://github.com/brycewang-stanford/Awesome-Journal-Skills/tree/HEAD/ICSE-Skills/skills/icse-author-response) · ★1.4k · 2 copies)
- **icse-camera-ready**: Use when converting an accepted ICSE research-track paper into its final published form, covering the extra camera-ready page, de-anonymization order, finalizing the Data Availability section with archival DOIs, IEEE-template production checks, and conference-presentation obligations to verify. ([brycewang-stanford/Awesome-Journal-Skills](https://github.com/brycewang-stanford/Awesome-Journal-Skills/tree/HEAD/ICSE-Skills/skills/icse-camera-ready) · ★1.4k · 2 copies)
- **icse-experiments**: Use when designing or auditing the evaluation of an ICSE research-track paper, covering subject and benchmark selection, baseline fairness, statistical tests and effect sizes as SE reviewers expect them, qualitative-methods rigor, ablations for AI-based techniques, and threats-driven study design. ([brycewang-stanford/Awesome-Journal-Skills](https://github.com/brycewang-stanford/Awesome-Journal-Skills/tree/HEAD/ICSE-Skills/skills/icse-experiments) · ★1.4k · 2 copies)
- **icse-related-work**: Use when building the related-work and positioning sections of an ICSE research-track paper, covering the literature lanes SE reviewers check, searching ACM DL, IEEE Xplore and dblp across the flagship venues, staying current in fast-moving areas like LLM4SE, and keeping self-citations double-anonymous. ([brycewang-stanford/Awesome-Journal-Skills](https://github.com/brycewang-stanford/Awesome-Journal-Skills/tree/HEAD/ICSE-Skills/skills/icse-related-work) · ★1.4k · 2 copies)
- **icse-reproducibility**: Use when building the verifiability story of an ICSE research-track paper, covering the open-science policy's sharing-by-default expectation, the Data Availability section, anonymized replication packages at review time, provenance pinning for mining and LLM studies, and honest non-sharing justifications. ([brycewang-stanford/Awesome-Journal-Skills](https://github.com/brycewang-stanford/Awesome-Journal-Skills/tree/HEAD/ICSE-Skills/skills/icse-reproducibility) · ★1.4k · 2 copies)
- **icse-review-process**: Use when reasoning about how an ICSE research-track submission is evaluated, covering double-anonymous PC review, the four posted criteria, the Accept / Major Revision / Reject decision model, the revision-and-final-decision timeline, and what recent ICSE acceptance statistics imply for strategy. ([brycewang-stanford/Awesome-Journal-Skills](https://github.com/brycewang-stanford/Awesome-Journal-Skills/tree/HEAD/ICSE-Skills/skills/icse-review-process) · ★1.4k · 2 copies)
- **icse-seet-review**: ICSE SEET软件工程教育与培训论文评审技能。用于评审Software Engineering Education and Training教育类论文、ICSE SEET投稿或类似软件工程教育研究/经验/工具论文；当用户要求SEET review、教育类顶会论文评审、按Relevance/Significance/Soundness/Verifiability/Presentation标准写评审意见、分析教育研究论文是否达到ICSE SEET标准、生成机器可读Markdown和给人看的图文并茂HTML时使用。该技能强调先判定论文类型，再按ICSE SEET对应类别标准做证据化评审，将主要不足从逐项标准风险中提炼，并分章输出主要优点、主要不足、修改建议、正式评语、评分/结果和可视化报告。 ([yylonly/buaa-thesis-checker](https://github.com/yylonly/buaa-thesis-checker/tree/HEAD/skills/icse-seet-review))
- **icse-submission**: Use when auditing an ICSE research-track submission for HotCRP readiness, covering the mandatory abstract deadline, the 10-page-plus-2-reference-page IEEE format, double-anonymous rules, the open-science Data Availability section, supplementary uploads, and desk-reject triage before the AoE cutoff. ([brycewang-stanford/Awesome-Journal-Skills](https://github.com/brycewang-stanford/Awesome-Journal-Skills/tree/HEAD/ICSE-Skills/skills/icse-submission) · ★1.4k · 2 copies)
- **icse-supplementary**: Use when deciding what goes into the 10-page body versus the replication package of an ICSE research-track submission, covering the no-unlimited-appendix page model, anonymized supplementary links and HotCRP uploads, package organization for reviewer navigation, and content-placement rules that protect the review. ([brycewang-stanford/Awesome-Journal-Skills](https://github.com/brycewang-stanford/Awesome-Journal-Skills/tree/HEAD/ICSE-Skills/skills/icse-supplementary) · ★1.4k · 2 copies)
- **icse-topic-selection**: Use when deciding whether a software-engineering project belongs in the ICSE research track or should be routed to FSE, ASE, ISSTA, MSR, ICSME, EMSE/TSE/TOSEM, or an ICSE co-located track such as NIER, SEIP, SEIS, or Demonstrations, based on contribution type, evidence maturity, and audience. ([brycewang-stanford/Awesome-Journal-Skills](https://github.com/brycewang-stanford/Awesome-Journal-Skills/tree/HEAD/ICSE-Skills/skills/icse-topic-selection) · ★1.4k · 2 copies)
- **icse-workflow**: Use when planning an ICSE research-track campaign end to end, covering the single-cycle calendar from the June abstract deadline through September response, October decisions, the November revision sprint, artifact evaluation, camera-ready, and the April conference, plus rerouting timelines after rejection. ([brycewang-stanford/Awesome-Journal-Skills](https://github.com/brycewang-stanford/Awesome-Journal-Skills/tree/HEAD/ICSE-Skills/skills/icse-workflow) · ★1.4k · 2 copies)
- **icse-writing**: Write and revise software engineering papers targeting ICSE Research Track. Encodes structural conventions, narrative patterns, evidence boundaries, and writing style from verified ICSE 2025/2026 Research Track papers in SAST, LLM+program analysis, vulnerability detection, and false-positive mitigation. Use when the user asks to write, revise, restructure, or polish a paper targeting ICSE, or when they mention ICSE Research Track, SE conference writing, or need ICSE-style structural guidance. ([wildoranges/icse-writing](https://github.com/wildoranges/icse-writing/blob/HEAD/SKILL.md) · ★3)
- **icse-writing-style**: Use when writing or revising an ICSE research-track paper, covering empirical-SE paper anatomy, research questions as the load-bearing structure, the threats-to-validity section the community expects, practitioner-grounded motivation, calibrated claims, and fitting a complete study into ten IEEE pages. ([brycewang-stanford/Awesome-Journal-Skills](https://github.com/brycewang-stanford/Awesome-Journal-Skills/tree/HEAD/ICSE-Skills/skills/icse-writing-style) · ★1.4k · 2 copies)
- **icsid-procedure-advisor**: icsid-procedure-advisor — 解决投资争端国际中心（ICSID）仲裁程序的全程指引，涵盖ICSID公约2022版仲裁规则下的案件受理、仲裁庭组成、管辖裁决与实体审理 ([vivy-yi/Greater-China-Legal](https://github.com/vivy-yi/Greater-China-Legal/tree/HEAD/plugins/legal-scenes/commercial-arbitration/skills/icsid-procedure-advisor) · ★50)

([ThomasMoreAI/legal-skills-open](https://github.com/ThomasMoreAI/legal-skills-open/tree/HEAD/cross-jurisdiction/commercial/skills/icsid-quellenkarte) · ★136)
- **icsid-quellenkarte**: Wenn es um Icsid Quellenkarte in Fachanwalt Internationales Wirtschaftsrecht geht: ordnet Sachverhalt, Norm, Beweislast, Gegenargumente und nächsten Schritt; liefert eine Tatbestands- oder Anspruchsmatrix mit Gegenargumenten. ([vincentagwu/deutsches-recht-slim](https://github.com/vincentagwu/deutsches-recht-slim/tree/HEAD/fachanwalt-internationales-wirtschaftsrecht/skills/icsid-quellenkarte))
- **icsm-adjust-testosterone-dose**: Advises adjusting testosterone dose based on serum levels and clinical response: reduce dose if total testosterone consistently exceeds the normal range, escalate if hypogonadal symptoms persist despite sub-mid-normal levels. Triggered when a clinician reviews a patient's testosterone result and symptoms and wonders, "Do I need to change the dose or is the current dose appropriate?" ([dromlakhani/MD2SKILL](https://github.com/dromlakhani/MD2SKILL/tree/HEAD/skills/endocrinology/hypogonadism/icsm-adjust-testosterone-dose) · ★14)
- **icsm-avoid-testosterone-acute-illness**: Advises against obtaining testosterone levels during acute illness as results may be unreliable. Triggers when a patient is febrile, infected, or otherwise acutely unwell and the clinician questions whether to draw testosterone now or delay testing until recovery. ([dromlakhani/MD2SKILL](https://github.com/dromlakhani/MD2SKILL/tree/HEAD/skills/endocrinology/hypogonadism/icsm-avoid-testosterone-acute-illness) · ★14)
- **icsm-avoid-tt-bcr**: Advises against testosterone therapy in men with biochemical recurrence after prostate cancer treatment due to very limited data and potential risk of progression. Consider when a patient has a rising PSA after definitive therapy and the clinician evaluates testosterone for hypogonadism, questioning whether TTh is safe in BCR. ([dromlakhani/MD2SKILL](https://github.com/dromlakhani/MD2SKILL/tree/HEAD/skills/endocrinology/hypogonadism/icsm-avoid-tt-bcr) · ★14)
- **icsm-avoid-tt-for-depressive-disorders**: Testosterone monotherapy is not an effective treatment for clinical depressive disorders in hypogonadal men and should not be used for that purpose. Consider this when a patient presents with depressive mood and low testosterone, questioning whether testosterone will improve depression or if depression should be treated separately. ([dromlakhani/MD2SKILL](https://github.com/dromlakhani/MD2SKILL/tree/HEAD/skills/endocrinology/hypogonadism/icsm-avoid-tt-for-depressive-disorders) · ★14)
- **icsm-avoid-tt-for-diabetes-prevention**: The guideline recommends against using testosterone therapy alone to prevent or treat diabetes in men with hypogonadism. Clinicians should consider this recommendation when contemplating testosterone to improve HbA1c or insulin resistance and questioning whether testosterone can replace glucose-lowering therapy or be used for diabetes management. ([dromlakhani/MD2SKILL](https://github.com/dromlakhani/MD2SKILL/tree/HEAD/skills/endocrinology/hypogonadism/icsm-avoid-tt-for-diabetes-prevention) · ★14)
- **icsm-avoid-tt-for-fathering**: This skill advises against initiating testosterone therapy in men who express a desire for future fathering, as exogenous testosterone suppresses spermatogenesis and can lead to infertility. It is triggered when a patient reports wanting children and the clinician contemplates prescribing testosterone, raising the question of whether testosterone therapy is safe for future fertility or should be avoided. ([dromlakhani/MD2SKILL](https://github.com/dromlakhani/MD2SKILL/tree/HEAD/skills/endocrinology/hypogonadism/icsm-avoid-tt-for-fathering) · ★14)
- **icsm-avoid-tt-for-fracture-risk-reduction**: Recommends against using testosterone monotherapy solely to reduce fracture risk in hypogonadal men with high fracture risk. Triggered when a clinician considers osteoporosis management and asks whether testosterone will prevent fractures or if testosterone therapy should be used for bone health. ([dromlakhani/MD2SKILL](https://github.com/dromlakhani/MD2SKILL/tree/HEAD/skills/endocrinology/hypogonadism/icsm-avoid-tt-for-fracture-risk-reduction) · ★14)
- **icsm-avoid-tt-for-weight-loss**: The skill advises against using testosterone therapy alone for weight loss or cardio-metabolic improvement. It triggers when a clinician considers prescribing testosterone for obesity or diabetes and questions whether testosterone alone will aid weight loss or improve metabolic parameters. ([dromlakhani/MD2SKILL](https://github.com/dromlakhani/MD2SKILL/tree/HEAD/skills/endocrinology/hypogonadism/icsm-avoid-tt-for-weight-loss) · ★14)
- **icsm-avoid-tt-in-eugonadal**: This skill advises against initiating testosterone therapy in eugonadal men with normal serum testosterone and no symptoms of deficiency. It is triggered when a clinician questions whether to prescribe testosterone for a patient with normal labs and no hypogonadal symptoms, such as asking 'should I give testosterone to someone with normal T?' or 'is treatment appropriate for eugonadal men?' ([dromlakhani/MD2SKILL](https://github.com/dromlakhani/MD2SKILL/tree/HEAD/skills/endocrinology/hypogonadism/icsm-avoid-tt-in-eugonadal) · ★14)
- **icsm-avoid-tt-metastatic**: Contraindicates testosterone therapy in men with metastatic prostate cancer because of insufficient safety data and risk of rapid progression. Triggers when a patient has metastatic disease and low testosterone and the clinician wonders "can I use testosterone here?" or "is testosterone therapy appropriate in metastatic PCa?" ([dromlakhani/MD2SKILL](https://github.com/dromlakhani/MD2SKILL/tree/HEAD/skills/endocrinology/hypogonadism/icsm-avoid-tt-metastatic) · ★14)
- **icsm-avoid-universal-testosterone-screening**: This skill advises against universal testosterone screening in asymptomatic men, recommending testing only when specific clinical indications are present. Trigger phrases include 'should I screen everyone for low T', 'is universal testing appropriate', or considering testosterone order in an asymptomatic patient. ([dromlakhani/MD2SKILL](https://github.com/dromlakhani/MD2SKILL/tree/HEAD/skills/endocrinology/hypogonadism/icsm-avoid-universal-testosterone-screening) · ★14)
- **icsm-calculate-free-testosterone-vermeulen**: Provides a practical algorithm to estimate free testosterone from total testosterone, SHBG, and albumin using the Vermeulen equation for clinical use when direct measurement is unavailable. Triggers when a clinician needs to assess bioavailable testosterone and asks "how do I calculate free T?" or "what free T value corresponds to this total T and SHBG?" ([dromlakhani/MD2SKILL](https://github.com/dromlakhani/MD2SKILL/tree/HEAD/skills/endocrinology/hypogonadism/icsm-calculate-free-testosterone-vermeulen) · ★14)
- **icsm-calculated-free-testosterone**: This skill suggests measuring albumin and SHBG to calculate free testosterone in men with clear dysmetabolic conditions to improve diagnostic accuracy. It is triggered when managing a patient with obesity, diabetes, or metabolic syndrome and questioning whether to calculate free testosterone or obtain SHBG and albumin. ([dromlakhani/MD2SKILL](https://github.com/dromlakhani/MD2SKILL/tree/HEAD/skills/endocrinology/hypogonadism/icsm-calculated-free-testosterone) · ★14)
- **icsm-choose-testosterone-assay-method**: Recommends mass spectrometry for research purposes and good-quality immunoassays for routine clinical diagnosis when standardized against an international reference range. Triggers when a clinician orders a testosterone test and asks "which assay should I use?" or questions "is this immunoassay reliable enough?" ([dromlakhani/MD2SKILL](https://github.com/dromlakhani/MD2SKILL/tree/HEAD/skills/endocrinology/hypogonadism/icsm-choose-testosterone-assay-method) · ★14)
- **icsm-consider-tt-active-surveillance-accept-risk**: Considers offering testosterone therapy to men on active surveillance for low-grade prostate cancer when both clinician and patient acknowledge the limited data on cancer progression risk. Triggered when managing a man with low-grade prostate cancer and low testosterone who questions whether testosterone can be used while monitoring the cancer. ([dromlakhani/MD2SKILL](https://github.com/dromlakhani/MD2SKILL/tree/HEAD/skills/endocrinology/hypogonadism/icsm-consider-tt-active-surveillance-accept-risk) · ★14)
- **icsm-consider-tt-active-surveillance-shared-understanding**: This skill advises that testosterone therapy may be reasonable for men on active surveillance for prostate cancer who have hypogonadism when both clinician and patient acknowledge the paucity of long-term safety data. It is triggered when a clinician manages a man on cancer surveillance with hypogonadism and questions whether it is okay to use testosterone while monitoring the cancer, and both clinician and patient accept the uncertainty. ([dromlakhani/MD2SKILL](https://github.com/dromlakhani/MD2SKILL/tree/HEAD/skills/endocrinology/hypogonadism/icsm-consider-tt-active-surveillance-shared-understanding) · ★14)
- **icsm-consider-tt-after-radiation-good-response**: This skill guides clinicians to consider testosterone therapy after definitive radiation or brachytherapy for prostate cancer when the patient shows a good post-treatment response (e.g., stable low PSA) and remains low-risk. It is triggered when a patient with persistent hypogonadism symptoms and low testosterone levels is being followed post-radiotherapy and the clinician questions the safety of initiating testosterone therapy. ([dromlakhani/MD2SKILL](https://github.com/dromlakhani/MD2SKILL/tree/HEAD/skills/endocrinology/hypogonadism/icsm-consider-tt-after-radiation-good-response) · ★14)
- **icsm-consider-tt-after-rp-undetectable-psa**: This skill suggests testosterone therapy may be considered after radical prostatectomy when postoperative PSA is undetectable and pathology reveals negative surgical margins, negative seminal vesicles, and negative lymph nodes. It is triggered when a clinician encounters a post‑prostatectomy patient with symptomatic low testosterone and questions whether it is safe to initiate testosterone therapy or if the cancer is sufficiently cleared. ([dromlakhani/MD2SKILL](https://github.com/dromlakhani/MD2SKILL/tree/HEAD/skills/endocrinology/hypogonadism/icsm-consider-tt-after-rp-undetectable-psa) · ★14)
- **icsm-consider-tt-normal-psa-no-pca**: Large RCTs show no increased prostate cancer risk with testosterone therapy in men without known prostate cancer and normal PSA, supporting its consideration in this population. This skill is triggered when a clinician evaluates a hypogonadal man with normal PSA and no prior cancer diagnosis and questions whether testosterone therapy is safe to initiate. ([dromlakhani/MD2SKILL](https://github.com/dromlakhani/MD2SKILL/tree/HEAD/skills/endocrinology/hypogonadism/icsm-consider-tt-normal-psa-no-pca) · ★14)
- **icsm-defer-tt-elevated-psa-or-nodules**: Recommends postponing testosterone initiation and pursuing MRI or biopsy when PSA is elevated or a prostate nodule is detected on digital rectal examination; if evaluation shows no cancer, testosterone therapy may be considered. Triggered by clinician findings of elevated PSA (&gt;4 ng/mL) or a palpable prostate nodule during DRE, prompting the question of whether to hold testosterone until prostate cancer is ruled out. ([dromlakhani/MD2SKILL](https://github.com/dromlakhani/MD2SKILL/tree/HEAD/skills/endocrinology/hypogonadism/icsm-defer-tt-elevated-psa-or-nodules) · ★14)
- **icsm-discuss-fertility-preservation-pre-tt**: Recommends a thorough conversation about options to preserve spermatogenesis and testicular volume when initiating testosterone in men with prior anabolic‑steroid use or ongoing testosterone therapy. Triggers when a clinician plans to start testosterone in a man with a history of AAS or current TTh and wonders how to protect fertility or what alternatives exist to maintain sperm production. ([dromlakhani/MD2SKILL](https://github.com/dromlakhani/MD2SKILL/tree/HEAD/skills/endocrinology/hypogonadism/icsm-discuss-fertility-preservation-pre-tt) · ★14)
- **icsm-dre-before-tt**: Perform digital rectal examination (DRE) in all men before initiating testosterone therapy to exclude prostate abnormalities or support suspicion of hypogonadism when prostate volume is reduced. Triggered when a clinician considers starting testosterone and questions whether a prostate exam is needed first or whether to check the prostate before prescribing TTh. ([dromlakhani/MD2SKILL](https://github.com/dromlakhani/MD2SKILL/tree/HEAD/skills/endocrinology/hypogonadism/icsm-dre-before-tt) · ★14)
- **icsm-estimate-tt-increase-from-bariatric-surgery**: Estimates that bariatric surgery can raise serum testosterone by approximately 10 nmol/L in obese hypogonadal patients. Triggered when a clinician considers surgical weight loss for a hypogonadal obese patient and wonders how much testosterone might rise after surgery or what T change to anticipate from bariatric procedures. ([dromlakhani/MD2SKILL](https://github.com/dromlakhani/MD2SKILL/tree/HEAD/skills/endocrinology/hypogonadism/icsm-estimate-tt-increase-from-bariatric-surgery) · ★14)
- **icsm-estimate-tt-increase-from-weight-loss**: Estimates the expected rise in serum testosterone following a 10% weight loss in obese hypogonadal men, using the approximation of a 2-4 nmol/L increase. Triggered when a clinician discusses lifestyle modification with an obese hypogonadal patient and questions the anticipated testosterone change from diet and exercise. ([dromlakhani/MD2SKILL](https://github.com/dromlakhani/MD2SKILL/tree/HEAD/skills/endocrinology/hypogonadism/icsm-estimate-tt-increase-from-weight-loss) · ★14)
- **icsm-hematocrit-monitoring-tt**: This skill guides clinicians to maintain hematocrit below 54% before and during testosterone therapy, with particular attention to high-altitude populations to mitigate polycythemia risk. It is triggered when reviewing labs and asking, "Is the hematocrit safe or do I need to adjust or hold testosterone due to high Hct?" ([dromlakhani/MD2SKILL](https://github.com/dromlakhani/MD2SKILL/tree/HEAD/skills/endocrinology/hypogonadism/icsm-hematocrit-monitoring-tt) · ★14)
- **icsm-hypogonadism-diagnosis-confirmation**: This skill confirms the diagnosis of male hypogonadism by requiring both characteristic clinical symptoms and consistently low total testosterone levels on two separate morning measurements. It is triggered when a clinician evaluates a patient with suspected hypogonadism and wonders whether low testosterone and symptoms are present or if the diagnosis should be confirmed. ([dromlakhani/MD2SKILL](https://github.com/dromlakhani/MD2SKILL/tree/HEAD/skills/endocrinology/hypogonadism/icsm-hypogonadism-diagnosis-confirmation) · ★14)
- **icsm-indications-for-testosterone-assessment**: Recommends evaluating testosterone in men presenting with sexual symptoms (low libido, erectile dysfunction, decreased morning erections), metabolic conditions (obesity, type 2 diabetes, metabolic syndrome), HIV infection, opioid or glucocorticoid use, bone density loss, or male infertility. Triggers when a clinician encounters a patient with any of these indications and considers whether to check testosterone levels. ([dromlakhani/MD2SKILL](https://github.com/dromlakhani/MD2SKILL/tree/HEAD/skills/endocrinology/hypogonadism/icsm-indications-for-testosterone-assessment) · ★14)
- **icsm-morning-testosterone-measurement**: Recommends measuring total testosterone in the morning fasting state (07:00–11:00) using a reliable assay for patients suspected of hypogonadism. Triggered when a clinician orders a testosterone test and questions whether the sample should be drawn fasting and in the morning. ([dromlakhani/MD2SKILL](https://github.com/dromlakhani/MD2SKILL/tree/HEAD/skills/endocrinology/hypogonadism/icsm-morning-testosterone-measurement) · ★14)
- **icsm-offer-tt-after-radiation-low-psa-consent**: This skill guides offering testosterone therapy after radiation or brachytherapy when a patient's PSA has fallen to a low level, typically at 3-6 months post-treatment. It is triggered when clinicians consider initiating testosterone and seek confirmation that the PSA is sufficiently low, accompanied by a consent form discussing uncertainties. ([dromlakhani/MD2SKILL](https://github.com/dromlakhani/MD2SKILL/tree/HEAD/skills/endocrinology/hypogonadism/icsm-offer-tt-after-radiation-low-psa-consent) · ★14)
- **icsm-offer-tt-after-rp-favorable-pathology-undetectable-psa**: This skill guides clinicians to consider testosterone therapy in postoperative radical prostatectomy patients who have undetectable PSA and favorable pathology (negative margins, seminal vesicles, and lymph nodes). Trigger when a postoperative patient reports recovered undetectable PSA and pathology is clear, raising the question of initiating testosterone replacement. ([dromlakhani/MD2SKILL](https://github.com/dromlakhani/MD2SKILL/tree/HEAD/skills/endocrinology/hypogonadism/icsm-offer-tt-after-rp-favorable-pathology-undetectable-psa) · ★14)
- **icsm-physical-exam-hypogonadism-signs**: Advises checking for smaller testes, decreased body hair, and gynecomastia as physical clues to hypogonadism. Triggered when evaluating a man with suspected testosterone deficiency who asks what physical exam findings support the diagnosis or whether any exam signs suggest hypogonadism. ([dromlakhani/MD2SKILL](https://github.com/dromlakhani/MD2SKILL/tree/HEAD/skills/endocrinology/hypogonadism/icsm-physical-exam-hypogonadism-signs) · ★14)
- **icsm-prostate-monitoring-tt**: Recommends prostate-specific antigen (PSA) testing and digital rectal examination (DRE) to monitor prostate health during testosterone therapy, following evidence-based guidelines for prostate cancer surveillance. - **idas4you-doc**: 작업지시서(설계·계획·작업 체크리스트, 필요하면 ADR)를 쓰거나 고친다. 코드는 읽기만 하고 고치지 않는다. "작업지시서 써줘", "지시서 고쳐줘", "/idas4you-doc &lt;주제&gt;"일 때 쓴다. 다 쓴 지시서의 검수는 /idas4you-doc-check. ([idas4you/claude-plugins](https://github.com/idas4you/claude-plugins/tree/HEAD/plugins/workdoc/skills/idas4you-doc))
- **idas4you-doc-check**: /idas4you-doc이 쓴 작업지시서(design.md·plan.md·tasks.md·ADR)를 구현 전에 검수하고, 구현에 쓸 모델을 추천한다. 코드는 읽기만 한다. "지시서 검수해줘", "지시서에 문제 없는지 봐줘", "구현 모델 추천해줘", "/idas4you-doc-check &lt;지시서 디렉터리&gt;"일 때 쓴다. ([idas4you/claude-plugins](https://github.com/idas4you/claude-plugins/tree/HEAD/plugins/workdoc/skills/idas4you-doc-check))
- **idas4you-doc-check-all**: 아직 검수하지 않은 작업지시서를 모두 찾아 한 번에 검수한다. 검수 기준은 /idas4you-doc-check와 같고, 도중에 묻지 않는다 — 결정이 필요한 것은 각 지시서의 "미결 질문"에 권장안과 함께 적어 두고 /idas4you-doc-impl이 구현 전에 묻게 한다. "지시서 전부 검수해줘", "검수 안 한 거 다 봐줘", "/idas4you-doc-check-all"일 때 쓴다. 하나만 검수하려면 /idas4you-doc-check &lt;지시서 디렉터리&gt;. ([idas4you/claude-plugins](https://github.com/idas4you/claude-plugins/tree/HEAD/plugins/workdoc/skills/idas4you-doc-check-all))
- **idas4you-doc-done**: 구현이 끝난 작업지시서를 닫는다 — 닫아도 되는지 확인하고, 지시서·인덱스·ADR의 상태를 `완료`로 바꾸고, 이 지시서에 막혀 있던 지시서를 푼다. `중단`·`대체됨`으로 닫는 것도 한다. 코드는 고치지 않고 머지·배포도 하지 않는다. "지시서 완료 처리해줘", "이 작업 닫아줘", "지시서 중단 처리해줘", "/idas4you-doc-done &lt;지시서 디렉터리&gt;"일 때 쓴다. ([idas4you/claude-plugins](https://github.com/idas4you/claude-plugins/tree/HEAD/plugins/workdoc/skills/idas4you-doc-done))
- **idas4you-doc-impl**: 작성된 작업지시서(design.md·tasks.md)를 구현하고 시험한다. 기능 브랜치에서 코드·테스트·측정을 하고 결과를 지시서에 기록한다. tasks.md에 `구현 모델 권장`이 있으면 그 모델로 구현한다. 지시서 경로 없이 부르면 인덱스의 우선순위(상·중·하)로 다음 지시서를 골라 제안한다. "구현해줘", "지시서대로 진행해줘", "다음 거 구현해줘", "/idas4you-doc-impl &lt;지시서 경로&gt;"일 때 쓴다. ([idas4you/claude-plugins](https://github.com/idas4you/claude-plugins/tree/HEAD/plugins/workdoc/skills/idas4you-doc-impl))
- **idas4you-doc-impl-list**: 끝나지 않은 작업지시서를 우선순위 순으로 목록으로 보여 주고, 선택 화면에서 고른 지시서의 구현을 바로 시작한다. 목록만 보고 고르지 않을 수도 있다. "지시서 목록 보여줘", "구현할 거 골라볼게", "남은 지시서 뭐 있지", "/idas4you-doc-impl-list"일 때 쓴다. 지시서를 이미 정했으면 /idas4you-doc-impl &lt;지시서 디렉터리&gt;. ([idas4you/claude-plugins](https://github.com/idas4you/claude-plugins/tree/HEAD/plugins/workdoc/skills/idas4you-doc-impl-list))
- **idas4you-session-check**: 세션을 끝내기 전에 남은 것을 찾아 목록으로 보여 준다 — 커밋 안 된 변경, push 안 된 커밋, 머지됐는데 남은 브랜치·워크트리, 뒤처진 기본 브랜치, 갱신 안 된 설치 플러그인. 찾기만 하고, 치우는 것은 묻고 나서 한다. "뒷정리 점검해줘", "남은 거 있나 봐줘", "끝내기 전에 확인해줘", "/idas4you-session-check"일 때 쓴다. 세션 내용을 정리하는 것은 /idas4you-session-summary. ([idas4you/claude-plugins](https://github.com/idas4you/claude-plugins/tree/HEAD/plugins/session/skills/idas4you-session-check))
- **idas4you-session-rename**: 이 세션의 대화를 바탕으로 세션(채팅) 제목만 바꾼다. 정리 메시지도, 정리 파일도 남기지 않는다. "채팅 이름 바꿔줘", "세션 제목 정해줘", "제목만 바꿔줘", "/idas4you-session-rename [제목]"일 때 쓴다. 세션에서 한 일까지 정리하려면 /idas4you-session-summary. ([idas4you/claude-plugins](https://github.com/idas4you/claude-plugins/tree/HEAD/plugins/session/skills/idas4you-session-rename))
- **idas4you-session-resume**: 지난 세션을 이어받는다 — /idas4you-session-summary가 ~/.claude/session-notes에 남긴 정리 중 이 저장소의 "남은 일"이 있는 것을 찾아, 지금 실제 상태와 대조해 보여 주고 무엇부터 할지 제안한다. 작업을 직접 시작하지는 않는다.
